+<span class="fc" id="L14">public class PersonDao {</span>
+
+    public Person authenticate(String username, String password) {
+        // Legacy: plaintext password comparison in SQL.
+<span class="fc" id="L18">        String sql = &quot;SELECT id, username, full_name, role FROM person WHERE username = ? AND password = ?&quot;;</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L19">        try (Connection c = Database.getConnection();</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L20">             PreparedStatement ps = c.prepareStatement(sql)) {</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L21">            ps.setString(1, username);</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L22">            ps.setString(2, password);</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L23">            try (ResultSet rs = ps.executeQuery()) {</span>
+<span class="fc bfc" id="L24" title="All 2 branches covered.">                return rs.next() ? map(rs) : null;</span>
+            }
+<span class="nc" id="L26">        } catch (SQLException e) {</span>
+<span class="nc" id="L27">            throw new RuntimeException(e);</span>
+        }
+    }
+
+    public List&lt;Person&gt; findAll() {
+<span class="fc" id="L32">        List&lt;Person&gt; out = new ArrayList&lt;&gt;();</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L33">        String sql = &quot;SELECT id, username, full_name, role FROM person ORDER BY full_name&quot;;</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L34">        try (Connection c = Database.getConnection();</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L35">             PreparedStatement ps = c.prepareStatement(sql);</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L36">             ResultSet rs = ps.executeQuery()) {</span>
+<span class="fc bfc" id="L37" title="All 2 branches covered.">            while (rs.next()) {</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L38">                out.add(map(rs));</span>
+            }
+<span class="nc" id="L40">        } catch (SQLException e) {</span>
+<span class="nc" id="L41">            throw new RuntimeException(e);</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L42">        }</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L43">        return out;</span>
+    }
+
+    private Person map(ResultSet rs) throws SQLException {
+<span class="fc" id="L47">        Person p = new Person();</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L48">        p.setId(rs.getLong(&quot;id&quot;));</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L49">        p.setUsername(rs.getString(&quot;username&quot;));</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L50">        p.setFullName(rs.getString(&quot;full_name&quot;));</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L51">        p.setRole(Role.valueOf(rs.getString(&quot;role&quot;)));</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L52">        return p;</span>
+    }

+import com.acme.tracker.db.Database;
+import com.acme.tracker.model.Project;
+
+import java.sql.Connection;
+import java.sql.PreparedStatement;
+import java.sql.ResultSet;
+import java.sql.SQLException;
+import java.util.ArrayList;
+import java.util.List;
+
+<span class="fc" id="L13">public class ProjectDao {</span>
+
+    private static final String BASE =
+        &quot;SELECT p.id, p.name, p.description, p.state, p.owner_id, o.full_name AS owner_name &quot; +
+        &quot;FROM project p JOIN person o ON o.id = p.owner_id&quot;;
+
+    public List&lt;Project&gt; findAll() {
+<span class="fc" id="L20">        List&lt;Project&gt; out = new ArrayList&lt;&gt;();</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L21">        try (Connection c = Database.getConnection();</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L22">             PreparedStatement ps = c.prepareStatement(BASE + &quot; ORDER BY p.name&quot;);</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L23">             ResultSet rs = ps.executeQuery()) {</span>
+<span class="fc bfc" id="L24" title="All 2 branches covered.">            while (rs.next()) {</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L25">                out.add(map(rs));</span>
+            }
+<span class="nc" id="L27">        } catch (SQLException e) {</span>
+<span class="nc" id="L28">            throw new RuntimeException(e);</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L29">        }</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L30">        return out;</span>
+    }
+
+    public Project findById(long id) {
+<span class="nc" id="L34">        try (Connection c = Database.getConnection();</span>
+<span class="nc" id="L35">             PreparedStatement ps = c.prepareStatement(BASE + &quot; WHERE p.id = ?&quot;)) {</span>
+<span class="nc" id="L36">            ps.setLong(1, id);</span>
+<span class="nc" id="L37">            try (ResultSet rs = ps.executeQuery()) {</span>
+<span class="nc bnc" id="L38" title="All 2 branches missed.">                return rs.next() ? map(rs) : null;</span>
+            }
+<span class="nc" id="L40">        } catch (SQLException e) {</span>
+<span class="nc" id="L41">            throw new RuntimeException(e);</span>
+        }
+    }
+
+    public boolean exists(long id) {
+<span class="fc" id="L46">        try (Connection c = Database.getConnection();</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L47">             PreparedStatement ps = c.prepareStatement(&quot;SELECT 1 FROM project WHERE id = ?&quot;)) {</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L48">            ps.setLong(1, id);</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L49">            try (ResultSet rs = ps.executeQuery()) {</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L50">                return rs.next();</span>
+            }
+<span class="nc" id="L52">        } catch (SQLException e) {</span>
+<span class="nc" id="L53">            throw new RuntimeException(e);</span>
+        }
+    }
+
+    public long create(Project p) {
+<span class="fc" id="L58">        String sql = &quot;INSERT INTO project (name, description, state, owner_id) VALUES (?, ?, 'ACTIVE', ?)&quot;;</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L59">        try (Connection c = Database.getConnection();</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L60">             PreparedStatement ps = c.prepareStatement(sql, PreparedStatement.RETURN_GENERATED_KEYS)) {</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L61">            ps.setString(1, p.getName());</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L62">            ps.setString(2, p.getDescription());</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L63">            ps.setLong(3, p.getOwnerId());</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L64">            ps.executeUpdate();</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L65">            try (ResultSet keys = ps.getGeneratedKeys()) {</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L66">                keys.next();</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L67">                return keys.getLong(&quot;id&quot;);</span>
+            }
+<span class="nc" id="L69">        } catch (SQLException e) {</span>
+<span class="nc" id="L70">            throw new RuntimeException(e);</span>
+        }
+    }
+
+    /** Moves a project from ACTIVE to ARCHIVED. */
+    public void archive(long id) {
+<span class="fc" id="L76">        String sql = &quot;UPDATE project SET state = 'ARCHIVED' WHERE id = ? AND state = 'ACTIVE'&quot;;</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L77">        try (Connection c = Database.getConnection();</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L78">             PreparedStatement ps = c.prepareStatement(sql)) {</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L79">            ps.setLong(1, id);</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L80">            ps.executeUpdate();</span>
+<span class="nc" id="L81">        } catch (SQLException e) {</span>
+<span class="nc" id="L82">            throw new RuntimeException(e);</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L83">        }</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L84">    }</span>
+
+    private Project map(ResultSet rs) throws SQLException {
+<span class="fc" id="L87">        Project p = new Project();</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L88">        p.setId(rs.getLong(&quot;id&quot;));</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L89">        p.setName(rs.getString(&quot;name&quot;));</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L90">        p.setDescription(rs.getString(&quot;description&quot;));</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L91">        p.setState(rs.getString(&quot;state&quot;));</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L92">        p.setOwnerId(rs.getLong(&quot;owner_id&quot;));</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L93">        p.setOwnerName(rs.getString(&quot;owner_name&quot;));</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L94">        return p;</span>
+    }
+}

+    private static final String BASE =
+        &quot;SELECT t.id, t.title, t.description, t.status, t.priority, t.assignee_id, &quot; +
+        &quot;       t.project_id, t.due_date, t.updated_at, a.full_name AS assignee_name, p.name AS project_name &quot; +
+        &quot;FROM task t &quot; +
+        &quot;JOIN project p ON p.id = t.project_id &quot; +
+        &quot;LEFT JOIN person a ON a.id = t.assignee_id&quot;;

+<span class="fc" id="L23">        return run(BASE + &quot; ORDER BY t.updated_at DESC&quot;);</span>

+    public Task findById(long id) {
+<span class="fc" id="L27">        List&lt;Task&gt; rows = runWith(BASE + &quot; WHERE t.id = ?&quot;, id);</span>

+    /**
+     * Finds tasks; the status and assignee filters are optional and the WHERE
+     * clause is assembled dynamically. Values are parameterized; only the sort
+     * column is concatenated (whitelisted), the classic legacy shortcut.
+     */
+    public List&lt;Task&gt; search(String status, Long assigneeId, String sort) {
+<span class="fc" id="L41">        StringBuilder sql = new StringBuilder(BASE);</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L42">        List&lt;Object&gt; params = new ArrayList&lt;&gt;();</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L43">        List&lt;String&gt; where = new ArrayList&lt;&gt;();</span>
+<span class="pc bpc" id="L44" title="1 of 4 branches missed.">        if (status != null &amp;&amp; !status.isEmpty()) {</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L45">            where.add(&quot;t.status = ?&quot;);</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L46">            params.add(status);</span>
+        }
+<span class="fc bfc" id="L48" title="All 2 branches covered.">        if (assigneeId != null) {</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L49">            where.add(&quot;t.assignee_id = ?&quot;);</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L50">            params.add(assigneeId);</span>
+        }
+<span class="fc bfc" id="L52" title="All 2 branches covered.">        if (!where.isEmpty()) {</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L53">            sql.append(&quot; WHERE &quot;).append(String.join(&quot; AND &quot;, where));</span>
+        }
+        // Whitelist the sort expression to avoid injection via the concatenation.
+<span class="fc" id="L56">        sql.append(&quot; ORDER BY &quot;).append(orderClause(sort));</span>
+
+<span class="fc" id="L58">        try (Connection c = Database.getConnection();</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L59">             PreparedStatement ps = c.prepareStatement(sql.toString())) {</span>
+<span class="fc bfc" id="L60" title="All 2 branches covered.">            for (int i = 0; i &lt; params.size(); i++) {</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L61">                ps.setObject(i + 1, params.get(i));</span>
+            }
+<span class="fc" id="L63">            try (ResultSet rs = ps.executeQuery()) {</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L64">                List&lt;Task&gt; out = new ArrayList&lt;&gt;();</span>
+<span class="fc bfc" id="L65" title="All 2 branches covered.">                while (rs.next()) {</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L66">                    out.add(map(rs));</span>
+                }
+<span class="fc" id="L68">                return out;</span>
+            }
+<span class="nc" id="L70">        } catch (SQLException e) {</span>
+<span class="nc" id="L71">            throw new RuntimeException(e);</span>
+        }
+    }

+    public long create(Task t) {
+<span class="fc" id="L76">        String sql = &quot;INSERT INTO task (title, description, status, priority, assignee_id, project_id, due_date) &quot; +</span>
+                     &quot;VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)&quot;;
+<span class="fc" id="L78">        try (Connection c = Database.getConnection();</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L79">             PreparedStatement ps = c.prepareStatement(sql, PreparedStatement.RETURN_GENERATED_KEYS)) {</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L80">            ps.setString(1, t.getTitle());</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L81">            ps.setString(2, t.getDescription());</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L82">            ps.setString(3, t.getStatus());</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L83">            ps.setString(4, t.getPriority());</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L84">            setNullableLong(ps, 5, t.getAssigneeId());</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L85">            ps.setLong(6, t.getProjectId());</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L86">            setNullableDate(ps, 7, t.getDueDate());</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L87">            ps.executeUpdate();</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L88">            try (ResultSet keys = ps.getGeneratedKeys()) {</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L89">                keys.next();</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L90">                return keys.getLong(&quot;id&quot;);</span>
+            }

+<span class="nc" id="L93">            throw new RuntimeException(e);</span>

+    /** Sets status DONE for many ids in a single UPDATE statement. */
+    public int bulkClose(long[] ids) {
+<span class="pc bpc" id="L129" title="2 of 4 branches missed.">        if (ids == null || ids.length == 0) {</span>
+<span class="nc" id="L130">            return 0;</span>
+        }
+<span class="fc" id="L132">        StringBuilder in = new StringBuilder();</span>
+<span class="fc bfc" id="L133" title="All 2 branches covered.">        for (int i = 0; i &lt; ids.length; i++) {</span>
+<span class="fc bfc" id="L134" title="All 2 branches covered.">            in.append(i == 0 ? &quot;?&quot; : &quot;,?&quot;);</span>
+        }
+<span class="fc" id="L136">        String sql = &quot;UPDATE task SET status = 'DONE', updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP &quot; +</span>
+                     &quot;WHERE status &lt;&gt; 'DONE' AND id IN (&quot; + in + &quot;)&quot;;
+<span class="fc" id="L138">        try (Connection c = Database.getConnection();</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L139">             PreparedStatement ps = c.prepareStatement(sql)) {</span>
+<span class="fc bfc" id="L140" title="All 2 branches covered.">            for (int i = 0; i &lt; ids.length; i++) {</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L141">                ps.setLong(i + 1, ids[i]);</span>
+            }
+<span class="fc" id="L143">            return ps.executeUpdate();</span>
+<span class="nc" id="L144">        } catch (SQLException e) {</span>
+<span class="nc" id="L145">            throw new RuntimeException(e);</span>
+        }

+    /**
+     * Whitelisted ORDER BY expression. `sort` is a UI token (updated/status/
+     * priority/due), never a column name from the request — mapped to a fixed
+     * clause here. Default is most-recently-updated first.
+     */
+    private static String orderClause(String sort) {
+<span class="fc bfc" id="L155" title="All 2 branches covered.">        if (&quot;status&quot;.equals(sort)) {</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L156">            return &quot;t.status, t.updated_at DESC&quot;;</span>
+        }
+<span class="fc bfc" id="L158" title="All 2 branches covered.">        if (&quot;priority&quot;.equals(sort)) {</span>
+            // Semantic order HIGH &gt; MEDIUM &gt; LOW (not alphabetical).
+<span class="fc" id="L160">            return &quot;CASE t.priority WHEN 'HIGH' THEN 0 WHEN 'MEDIUM' THEN 1 ELSE 2 END, t.updated_at DESC&quot;;</span>
+        }
+<span class="fc bfc" id="L162" title="All 4 branches covered.">        if (&quot;due&quot;.equals(sort) || &quot;due_date&quot;.equals(sort)) {</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L163">            return &quot;t.due_date, t.updated_at DESC&quot;;</span>
+        }
+<span class="fc bfc" id="L165" title="All 2 branches covered.">        if (&quot;title&quot;.equals(sort)) {</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L166">            return &quot;t.title, t.id&quot;;</span>
+        }
+<span class="fc bfc" id="L168" title="All 2 branches covered.">        if (&quot;id&quot;.equals(sort)) {</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L169">            return &quot;t.id&quot;;</span>
+        }
+<span class="fc" id="L171">        return &quot;t.updated_at DESC&quot;; // default, and explicit &quot;updated&quot;</span>
+    }

+    private static void setNullableLong(PreparedStatement ps, int idx, Long v) throws SQLException {
+<span class="fc bfc" id="L175" title="All 2 branches covered.">        if (v == null) {</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L176">            ps.setNull(idx, java.sql.Types.BIGINT);</span>
+        } else {
+<span class="fc" id="L178">            ps.setLong(idx, v);</span>
+        }
+<span class="fc" id="L180">    }</span>
+
+    private static void setNullableDate(PreparedStatement ps, int idx, String iso) throws SQLException {
+<span class="fc bfc" id="L183" title="All 4 branches covered.">        if (iso == null || iso.isEmpty()) {</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L184">            ps.setNull(idx, java.sql.Types.DATE);</span>
+        } else {
+<span class="fc" id="L186">            ps.setDate(idx, java.sql.Date.valueOf(iso));</span>
+        }
+<span class="fc" id="L188">    }</span>

+    private List&lt;Task&gt; run(String sql) {
+<span class="fc" id="L191">        try (Connection c = Database.getConnection();</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L192">             PreparedStatement ps = c.prepareStatement(sql);</span>

+<span class="fc" id="L196">                out.add(map(rs));</span>

+    private List&lt;Task&gt; runWith(String sql, long arg) {
+<span class="fc" id="L205">        try (Connection c = Database.getConnection();</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L206">             PreparedStatement ps = c.prepareStatement(sql)) {</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L207">            ps.setLong(1, arg);</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L208">            try (ResultSet rs = ps.executeQuery()) {</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L209">                List&lt;Task&gt; out = new ArrayList&lt;&gt;();</span>
+<span class="fc bfc" id="L210" title="All 2 branches covered.">                while (rs.next()) {</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L211">                    out.add(map(rs));</span>
+                }
+<span class="fc" id="L213">                return out;</span>

+<span class="nc" id="L215">        } catch (SQLException e) {</span>
+<span class="nc" id="L216">            throw new RuntimeException(e);</span>
+        }

+    private Task map(ResultSet rs) throws SQLException {
+<span class="fc" id="L221">        Task t = new Task();</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L222">        t.setId(rs.getLong(&quot;id&quot;));</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L223">        t.setTitle(rs.getString(&quot;title&quot;));</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L224">        t.setDescription(rs.getString(&quot;description&quot;));</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L225">        t.setStatus(rs.getString(&quot;status&quot;));</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L226">        t.setPriority(rs.getString(&quot;priority&quot;));</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L227">        long aid = rs.getLong(&quot;assignee_id&quot;);</span>
+<span class="fc bfc" id="L228" title="All 2 branches covered.">        t.setAssigneeId(rs.wasNull() ? null : aid);</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L229">        t.setAssigneeName(rs.getString(&quot;assignee_name&quot;));</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L230">        t.setProjectId(rs.getLong(&quot;project_id&quot;));</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L231">        t.setProjectName(rs.getString(&quot;project_name&quot;));</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L232">        java.sql.Date d = rs.getDate(&quot;due_date&quot;);</span>
+<span class="fc bfc" id="L233" title="All 2 branches covered.">        t.setDueDate(d == null ? null : d.toString());</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L234">        java.sql.Timestamp u = rs.getTimestamp(&quot;updated_at&quot;);</span>
+        // Trim the fractional seconds: &quot;2026-07-17 14:23:05.123&quot; -&gt; &quot;2026-07-17 14:23:05&quot;
+<span class="pc bpc" id="L236" title="1 of 2 branches missed.">        t.setUpdatedAt(u == null ? null : u.toString().substring(0, 19));</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L237">        return t;</span>
+    }

+<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?><!DOCTYPE html PUBLIC "-//W3C//DTD XHTML 1.0 Strict//EN" "http://www.w3.org/TR/xhtml1/DTD/xhtml1-strict.dtd"><html xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" lang="en"><head><meta http-equiv="Content-Type" content="text/html;charset=UTF-8"/><link rel="stylesheet" href="../jacoco-resources/report.css" type="text/css"/><link rel="shortcut icon" href="../jacoco-resources/report.gif" type="image/gif"/><title>BootstrapListener.java</title><link rel="stylesheet" href="../jacoco-resources/prettify.css" type="text/css"/><script type="text/javascript" src="../jacoco-resources/prettify.js"></script></head><body onload="window['PR_TAB_WIDTH']=4;prettyPrint()"><div class="breadcrumb" id="breadcrumb"><span class="info"><a href="../jacoco-sessions.html" class="el_session">Sessions</a></span><a href="../index.html" class="el_report">Frozen task-tracker-demo Java</a> &gt; <a href="index.source.html" class="el_package">com.acme.tracker.db</a> &gt; <span class="el_source">BootstrapListener.java</span></div><h1>BootstrapListener.java</h1><pre class="source lang-java linenums">package com.acme.tracker.db;
+
+import javax.servlet.ServletContextEvent;
+import javax.servlet.ServletContextListener;
+
+/** Waits for the external database to be reachable on startup. Wired in web.xml. */
+<span class="fc" id="L7">public class BootstrapListener implements ServletContextListener {</span>
+
+    @Override
+    public void contextInitialized(ServletContextEvent sce) {
+        // Schema + seed are owned by the Postgres container; just wait for it.
+<span class="fc" id="L12">        Database.awaitReady();</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L13">    }</span>
+
+    @Override
+    public void contextDestroyed(ServletContextEvent sce) {
+        // no-op
+<span class="nc" id="L18">    }</span>
+}
+</pre><div class="footer"><span class="right">Created with <a href="http://www.jacoco.org/jacoco">JaCoCo</a> 0.8.12.202403310830</span></div></body></html>

+<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?><!DOCTYPE html PUBLIC "-//W3C//DTD XHTML 1.0 Strict//EN" "http://www.w3.org/TR/xhtml1/DTD/xhtml1-strict.dtd"><html xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" lang="en"><head><meta http-equiv="Content-Type" content="text/html;charset=UTF-8"/><link rel="stylesheet" href="../jacoco-resources/report.css" type="text/css"/><link rel="shortcut icon" href="../jacoco-resources/report.gif" type="image/gif"/><title>Database.java</title><link rel="stylesheet" href="../jacoco-resources/prettify.css" type="text/css"/><script type="text/javascript" src="../jacoco-resources/prettify.js"></script></head><body onload="window['PR_TAB_WIDTH']=4;prettyPrint()"><div class="breadcrumb" id="breadcrumb"><span class="info"><a href="../jacoco-sessions.html" class="el_session">Sessions</a></span><a href="../index.html" class="el_report">Frozen task-tracker-demo Java</a> &gt; <a href="index.source.html" class="el_package">com.acme.tracker.db</a> &gt; <span class="el_source">Database.java</span></div><h1>Database.java</h1><pre class="source lang-java linenums">package com.acme.tracker.db;
+
+import java.sql.Connection;
+import java.sql.DriverManager;
+import java.sql.SQLException;
+import java.sql.Statement;
+
+/**
+ * Thin JDBC plumbing over an external PostgreSQL database: no connection pool,
+ * no ORM, statics everywhere. Connection settings come from
+ * the environment (DB_URL / DB_USER / DB_PASSWORD) with compose-friendly
+ * defaults. The database owns its own schema (loaded by the Postgres container
+ * at init time) — the app never creates tables.
+ */
+public final class Database {
+
+<span class="fc" id="L17">    private static final String URL = env(&quot;DB_URL&quot;, &quot;jdbc:postgresql://db:5432/tracker&quot;);</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L18">    private static final String USER = env(&quot;DB_USER&quot;, &quot;tracker&quot;);</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L19">    private static final String PASSWORD = env(&quot;DB_PASSWORD&quot;, &quot;tracker&quot;);</span>
+
+    static {
+        // Old-school explicit driver registration.
+        try {
+<span class="fc" id="L24">            Class.forName(&quot;org.postgresql.Driver&quot;);</span>
+<span class="nc" id="L25">        } catch (ClassNotFoundException e) {</span>
+<span class="nc" id="L26">            throw new IllegalStateException(&quot;PostgreSQL driver not on classpath&quot;, e);</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L27">        }</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L28">    }</span>
+
+    private Database() {
+    }
+
+    public static Connection getConnection() throws SQLException {
+<span class="fc" id="L34">        return DriverManager.getConnection(URL, USER, PASSWORD);</span>
+    }
+
+    /**
+     * Block until the database accepts connections. Schema + seed are loaded by
+     * the Postgres container, not here — this only waits for it to be ready so
+     * the first request doesn't fail if the app raced ahead of the DB.
+     */
+    public static void awaitReady() {
+<span class="fc" id="L43">        SQLException last = null;</span>
+<span class="pc bpc" id="L44" title="1 of 2 branches missed.">        for (int attempt = 1; attempt &lt;= 30; attempt++) {</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L45">            try (Connection c = getConnection(); Statement s = c.createStatement()) {</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L46">                s.execute(&quot;SELECT 1&quot;);</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L47">                return;</span>
+<span class="nc" id="L48">            } catch (SQLException e) {</span>
+<span class="nc" id="L49">                last = e;</span>
+                try {
+<span class="nc" id="L51">                    Thread.sleep(1000);</span>
+<span class="nc" id="L52">                } catch (InterruptedException ie) {</span>
+<span class="nc" id="L53">                    Thread.currentThread().interrupt();</span>
+<span class="nc" id="L54">                    return;</span>
+<span class="nc" id="L55">                }</span>
+            }
+        }
+<span class="nc" id="L58">        throw new RuntimeException(&quot;Database not reachable at &quot; + URL, last);</span>
+    }
+
+    private static String env(String key, String def) {
+<span class="fc" id="L62">        String v = System.getenv(key);</span>
+<span class="pc bpc" id="L63" title="2 of 4 branches missed.">        return (v == null || v.isEmpty()) ? def : v;</span>
+    }
+}

+<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?><!DOCTYPE html PUBLIC "-//W3C//DTD XHTML 1.0 Strict//EN" "http://www.w3.org/TR/xhtml1/DTD/xhtml1-strict.dtd"><html xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" lang="en"><head><meta http-equiv="Content-Type" content="text/html;charset=UTF-8"/><link rel="stylesheet" href="../jacoco-resources/report.css" type="text/css"/><link rel="shortcut icon" href="../jacoco-resources/report.gif" type="image/gif"/><title>Role.java</title><link rel="stylesheet" href="../jacoco-resources/prettify.css" type="text/css"/><script type="text/javascript" src="../jacoco-resources/prettify.js"></script></head><body onload="window['PR_TAB_WIDTH']=4;prettyPrint()"><div class="breadcrumb" id="breadcrumb"><span class="info"><a href="../jacoco-sessions.html" class="el_session">Sessions</a></span><a href="../index.html" class="el_report">Frozen task-tracker-demo Java</a> &gt; <a href="index.source.html" class="el_package">com.acme.tracker.model</a> &gt; <span class="el_source">Role.java</span></div><h1>Role.java</h1><pre class="source lang-java linenums">package com.acme.tracker.model;
+
+/**
+ * The user roles. The same four values are also declared as a CHECK constraint
+ * in schema.sql (person.role), so they are spelled out in two places.
+ */
+<span class="fc" id="L7">public enum Role {</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L8">    ADMIN,</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L9">    MANAGER,</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L10">    MEMBER,</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L11">    VIEWER;</span>
+
+    /** Whether this role may approve or reject tasks. */
+    public boolean canApprove() {
+<span class="fc bfc" id="L15" title="All 4 branches covered.">        return this == ADMIN || this == MANAGER;</span>
+    }
+}

+
+<span class="fc" id="L3">public class Task {</span>
+    private long id;
+    private String title;
+    private String description;
+    private String status;       // OPEN | IN_PROGRESS | PENDING_APPROVAL | DONE
+    private String priority;     // LOW | MEDIUM | HIGH
+    private Long assigneeId;     // nullable
+    private String assigneeName; // transient, joined for display
+    private long projectId;
+    private String projectName;  // transient, joined for display
+    private String dueDate;      // ISO yyyy-MM-dd, nullable
+    private String updatedAt;    // yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss, refreshed on every write
+
+<span class="fc" id="L16">    public long getId() { return id; }</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L17">    public void setId(long id) { this.id = id; }</span>
+
+<span class="fc" id="L19">    public String getTitle() { return title; }</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L20">    public void setTitle(String title) { this.title = title; }</span>
+
+<span class="fc" id="L22">    public String getDescription() { return description; }</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L23">    public void setDescription(String description) { this.description = description; }</span>
+
+<span class="fc" id="L25">    public String getStatus() { return status; }</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L26">    public void setStatus(String status) { this.status = status; }</span>
+
+<span class="fc" id="L28">    public String getPriority() { return priority; }</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L29">    public void setPriority(String priority) { this.priority = priority; }</span>
+
+<span class="fc" id="L31">    public Long getAssigneeId() { return assigneeId; }</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L32">    public void setAssigneeId(Long assigneeId) { this.assigneeId = assigneeId; }</span>
+
+<span class="fc" id="L34">    public String getAssigneeName() { return assigneeName; }</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L35">    public void setAssigneeName(String assigneeName) { this.assigneeName = assigneeName; }</span>
+
+<span class="fc" id="L37">    public long getProjectId() { return projectId; }</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L38">    public void setProjectId(long projectId) { this.projectId = projectId; }</span>
+
+<span class="fc" id="L40">    public String getProjectName() { return projectName; }</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L41">    public void setProjectName(String projectName) { this.projectName = projectName; }</span>

+<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?><!DOCTYPE html PUBLIC "-//W3C//DTD XHTML 1.0 Strict//EN" "http://www.w3.org/TR/xhtml1/DTD/xhtml1-strict.dtd"><html xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" lang="en"><head><meta http-equiv="Content-Type" content="text/html;charset=UTF-8"/><link rel="stylesheet" href="../jacoco-resources/report.css" type="text/css"/><link rel="shortcut icon" href="../jacoco-resources/report.gif" type="image/gif"/><title>ApiTaskServlet.java</title><link rel="stylesheet" href="../jacoco-resources/prettify.css" type="text/css"/><script type="text/javascript" src="../jacoco-resources/prettify.js"></script></head><body onload="window['PR_TAB_WIDTH']=4;prettyPrint()"><div class="breadcrumb" id="breadcrumb"><span class="info"><a href="../jacoco-sessions.html" class="el_session">Sessions</a></span><a href="../index.html" class="el_report">Frozen task-tracker-demo Java</a> &gt; <a href="index.source.html" class="el_package">com.acme.tracker.web</a> &gt; <span class="el_source">ApiTaskServlet.java</span></div><h1>ApiTaskServlet.java</h1><pre class="source lang-java linenums">package com.acme.tracker.web;
+
+import com.acme.tracker.dao.TaskDao;
+import com.acme.tracker.model.Task;
+
+import javax.servlet.http.HttpServlet;
+import javax.servlet.http.HttpServletRequest;
+import javax.servlet.http.HttpServletResponse;
+import java.io.IOException;
+import java.io.PrintWriter;
+import java.util.List;
+
+/**
+ * GET /api/tasks[?status=DONE] returns a JSON read-back of task rows straight
+ * from the DB, outside the UI path. JSON is hand-rolled (no Jackson) to keep
+ * the dependency surface legacy-thin.
+ */
+<span class="fc" id="L18">public class ApiTaskServlet extends HttpServlet {</span>
+
+<span class="fc" id="L20">    private final TaskDao tasks = new TaskDao();</span>
+
+    @Override
+    protected void doGet(HttpServletRequest req, HttpServletResponse resp) throws IOException {
+<span class="fc" id="L24">        String status = req.getParameter(&quot;status&quot;);</span>
+<span class="pc bpc" id="L25" title="1 of 4 branches missed.">        List&lt;Task&gt; rows = (status == null || status.isEmpty())</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L26">                ? tasks.findAll()</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L27">                : tasks.search(status, null, null);</span>
+
+<span class="fc" id="L29">        resp.setContentType(&quot;application/json&quot;);</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L30">        resp.setCharacterEncoding(&quot;UTF-8&quot;);</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L31">        PrintWriter out = resp.getWriter();</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L32">        out.append('[');</span>
+<span class="fc bfc" id="L33" title="All 2 branches covered.">        for (int i = 0; i &lt; rows.size(); i++) {</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L34">            Task t = rows.get(i);</span>
+<span class="fc bfc" id="L35" title="All 2 branches covered.">            if (i &gt; 0) out.append(',');</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L36">            out.append('{')</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L37">               .append(&quot;\&quot;id\&quot;:&quot;).append(String.valueOf(t.getId())).append(',')</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L38">               .append(&quot;\&quot;title\&quot;:\&quot;&quot;).append(esc(t.getTitle())).append(&quot;\&quot;,&quot;)</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L39">               .append(&quot;\&quot;status\&quot;:\&quot;&quot;).append(esc(t.getStatus())).append(&quot;\&quot;,&quot;)</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L40">               .append(&quot;\&quot;priority\&quot;:\&quot;&quot;).append(esc(t.getPriority())).append(&quot;\&quot;,&quot;)</span>
+<span class="fc bfc" id="L41" title="All 2 branches covered.">               .append(&quot;\&quot;assigneeId\&quot;:&quot;).append(t.getAssigneeId() == null ? &quot;null&quot; : String.valueOf(t.getAssigneeId())).append(',')</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L42">               .append(&quot;\&quot;projectId\&quot;:&quot;).append(String.valueOf(t.getProjectId())).append(',')</span>
+<span class="fc bfc" id="L43" title="All 2 branches covered.">               .append(&quot;\&quot;dueDate\&quot;:&quot;).append(t.getDueDate() == null ? &quot;null&quot; : &quot;\&quot;&quot; + esc(t.getDueDate()) + &quot;\&quot;&quot;).append(',')</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L44">               .append(&quot;\&quot;updatedAt\&quot;:\&quot;&quot;).append(esc(t.getUpdatedAt())).append(&quot;\&quot;&quot;)</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L45">               .append('}');</span>
+        }
+<span class="fc" id="L47">        out.append(']');</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L48">        out.flush();</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L49">    }</span>
+
+    private static String esc(String s) {
+<span class="pc bpc" id="L52" title="1 of 2 branches missed.">        if (s == null) return &quot;&quot;;</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L53">        return s.replace(&quot;\\&quot;, &quot;\\\\&quot;).replace(&quot;\&quot;&quot;, &quot;\\\&quot;&quot;);</span>
+    }
+}

+
+import com.acme.tracker.model.Person;
+
+import javax.servlet.Filter;
+import javax.servlet.FilterChain;
+import javax.servlet.ServletException;
+import javax.servlet.ServletRequest;
+import javax.servlet.ServletResponse;
+import javax.servlet.http.HttpServletRequest;
+import javax.servlet.http.HttpServletResponse;
+import javax.servlet.http.HttpSession;
+import java.io.IOException;
+
+/**
+ * Servlet filter applied to incoming requests (mapped to /* in web.xml).
+ */
+<span class="fc" id="L18">public class AuthFilter implements Filter {</span>
+
+    @Override
+    public void doFilter(ServletRequest req, ServletResponse res, FilterChain chain)
+            throws IOException, ServletException {
+<span class="fc" id="L23">        HttpServletRequest request = (HttpServletRequest) req;</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L24">        HttpServletResponse response = (HttpServletResponse) res;</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L25">        String path = request.getServletPath();</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L26">        String ctx = request.getContextPath();</span>
+
+<span class="pc bpc" id="L28" title="1 of 6 branches missed.">        if (path.equals(&quot;/login&quot;) || path.startsWith(&quot;/api/&quot;) || path.endsWith(&quot;.css&quot;)) {</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L29">            chain.doFilter(req, res);</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L30">            return;</span>
+        }
+
+<span class="fc" id="L33">        HttpSession session = request.getSession(false);</span>
+<span class="fc bfc" id="L34" title="All 2 branches covered.">        Person user = session == null ? null : (Person) session.getAttribute(&quot;user&quot;);</span>
+<span class="fc bfc" id="L35" title="All 2 branches covered.">        if (user == null) {</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L36">            response.sendRedirect(ctx + &quot;/login&quot;);</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L37">            return;</span>
+        }
+
+<span class="fc bfc" id="L40" title="All 2 branches covered.">        if (path.equals(&quot;/tasks/action&quot;)) {</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L41">            String action = request.getParameter(&quot;action&quot;);</span>
+<span class="fc bfc" id="L42" title="All 6 branches covered.">            if ((&quot;approve&quot;.equals(action) || &quot;reject&quot;.equals(action)) &amp;&amp; !user.getRole().canApprove()) {</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L43">                response.sendError(HttpServletResponse.SC_FORBIDDEN,</span>
+                        &quot;Only managers or admins may approve or reject tasks&quot;);
+<span class="fc" id="L45">                return;</span>
+            }
+        }
+
+<span class="fc" id="L49">        chain.doFilter(req, res);</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L50">    }</span>
+
+    @Override
+    public void init(javax.servlet.FilterConfig filterConfig) {
+<span class="fc" id="L54">    }</span>
+
+    @Override
+    public void destroy() {
+<span class="nc" id="L58">    }</span>
+}

+<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?><!DOCTYPE html PUBLIC "-//W3C//DTD XHTML 1.0 Strict//EN" "http://www.w3.org/TR/xhtml1/DTD/xhtml1-strict.dtd"><html xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" lang="en"><head><meta http-equiv="Content-Type" content="text/html;charset=UTF-8"/><link rel="stylesheet" href="../jacoco-resources/report.css" type="text/css"/><link rel="shortcut icon" href="../jacoco-resources/report.gif" type="image/gif"/><title>LoginServlet.java</title><link rel="stylesheet" href="../jacoco-resources/prettify.css" type="text/css"/><script type="text/javascript" src="../jacoco-resources/prettify.js"></script></head><body onload="window['PR_TAB_WIDTH']=4;prettyPrint()"><div class="breadcrumb" id="breadcrumb"><span class="info"><a href="../jacoco-sessions.html" class="el_session">Sessions</a></span><a href="../index.html" class="el_report">Frozen task-tracker-demo Java</a> &gt; <a href="index.source.html" class="el_package">com.acme.tracker.web</a> &gt; <span class="el_source">LoginServlet.java</span></div><h1>LoginServlet.java</h1><pre class="source lang-java linenums">package com.acme.tracker.web;
+
+import com.acme.tracker.dao.PersonDao;
+import com.acme.tracker.model.Person;
+
+import javax.servlet.ServletException;
+import javax.servlet.http.HttpServlet;
+import javax.servlet.http.HttpServletRequest;
+import javax.servlet.http.HttpServletResponse;
+import java.io.IOException;
+
+<span class="fc" id="L12">public class LoginServlet extends HttpServlet {</span>
+
+<span class="fc" id="L14">    private final PersonDao people = new PersonDao();</span>
+
+    @Override
+    protected void doGet(HttpServletRequest req, HttpServletResponse resp)
+            throws ServletException, IOException {
+<span class="fc" id="L19">        req.getRequestDispatcher(&quot;/WEB-INF/jsp/login.jsp&quot;).forward(req, resp);</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L20">    }</span>
+
+    @Override
+    protected void doPost(HttpServletRequest req, HttpServletResponse resp)
+            throws ServletException, IOException {
+<span class="fc" id="L25">        String username = req.getParameter(&quot;username&quot;);</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L26">        String password = req.getParameter(&quot;password&quot;);</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L27">        Person user = people.authenticate(username, password);</span>
+<span class="fc bfc" id="L28" title="All 2 branches covered.">        if (user == null) {</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L29">            req.setAttribute(&quot;error&quot;, &quot;Invalid credentials&quot;);</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L30">            req.getRequestDispatcher(&quot;/WEB-INF/jsp/login.jsp&quot;).forward(req, resp);</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L31">            return;</span>
+        }
+<span class="fc" id="L33">        req.getSession(true).setAttribute(&quot;user&quot;, user);</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L34">        resp.sendRedirect(req.getContextPath() + &quot;/tasks&quot;);</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L35">    }</span>
+}

+
+    @Override
+    protected void doGet(HttpServletRequest req, HttpServletResponse resp) throws IOException {
+<span class="fc" id="L13">        HttpSession session = req.getSession(false);</span>

+
+import com.acme.tracker.dao.PersonDao;
+import com.acme.tracker.dao.ProjectDao;
+import com.acme.tracker.model.Person;
+import com.acme.tracker.model.Project;
+
+import javax.servlet.ServletException;
+import javax.servlet.http.HttpServlet;
+import javax.servlet.http.HttpServletRequest;
+import javax.servlet.http.HttpServletResponse;
+import java.io.IOException;
+
+/**
+ * Lists projects, creates new ones, and archives an existing one.
+ */
+<span class="fc" id="L17">public class ProjectServlet extends HttpServlet {</span>
+
+<span class="fc" id="L19">    private final ProjectDao projects = new ProjectDao();</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L20">    private final PersonDao people = new PersonDao();</span>
+
+    @Override
+    protected void doGet(HttpServletRequest req, HttpServletResponse resp)
+            throws ServletException, IOException {
+<span class="fc" id="L25">        req.setAttribute(&quot;projects&quot;, projects.findAll());</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L26">        req.setAttribute(&quot;people&quot;, people.findAll());</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L27">        req.getRequestDispatcher(&quot;/WEB-INF/jsp/projects.jsp&quot;).forward(req, resp);</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L28">    }</span>
+
+    @Override
+    protected void doPost(HttpServletRequest req, HttpServletResponse resp) throws IOException {
+<span class="fc" id="L32">        String action = req.getParameter(&quot;action&quot;);</span>
+<span class="fc bfc" id="L33" title="All 2 branches covered.">        if (&quot;archive&quot;.equals(action)) {</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L34">            projects.archive(Long.parseLong(req.getParameter(&quot;id&quot;)));</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L35">            resp.sendRedirect(req.getContextPath() + &quot;/projects&quot;);</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L36">            return;</span>
+        }
+        // create
+<span class="fc" id="L39">        String name = req.getParameter(&quot;name&quot;);</span>
+<span class="pc bpc" id="L40" title="1 of 4 branches missed.">        if (name == null || name.trim().isEmpty()) {</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L41">            resp.sendError(HttpServletResponse.SC_BAD_REQUEST, &quot;Project name is required&quot;);</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L42">            return;</span>
+        }
+<span class="fc" id="L44">        Person user = (Person) req.getSession().getAttribute(&quot;user&quot;);</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L45">        Project p = new Project();</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L46">        p.setName(name.trim());</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L47">        p.setDescription(req.getParameter(&quot;description&quot;));</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L48">        p.setOwnerId(user.getId());</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L49">        projects.create(p);</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L50">        resp.sendRedirect(req.getContextPath() + &quot;/projects&quot;);</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L51">    }</span>
+}

+<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?><!DOCTYPE html PUBLIC "-//W3C//DTD XHTML 1.0 Strict//EN" "http://www.w3.org/TR/xhtml1/DTD/xhtml1-strict.dtd"><html xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" lang="en"><head><meta http-equiv="Content-Type" content="text/html;charset=UTF-8"/><link rel="stylesheet" href="../jacoco-resources/report.css" type="text/css"/><link rel="shortcut icon" href="../jacoco-resources/report.gif" type="image/gif"/><title>TaskActionServlet.java</title><link rel="stylesheet" href="../jacoco-resources/prettify.css" type="text/css"/><script type="text/javascript" src="../jacoco-resources/prettify.js"></script></head><body onload="window['PR_TAB_WIDTH']=4;prettyPrint()"><div class="breadcrumb" id="breadcrumb"><span class="info"><a href="../jacoco-sessions.html" class="el_session">Sessions</a></span><a href="../index.html" class="el_report">Frozen task-tracker-demo Java</a> &gt; <a href="index.source.html" class="el_package">com.acme.tracker.web</a> &gt; <span class="el_source">TaskActionServlet.java</span></div><h1>TaskActionServlet.java</h1><pre class="source lang-java linenums">package com.acme.tracker.web;
+
+import com.acme.tracker.dao.TaskDao;
+import com.acme.tracker.model.Person;
+import com.acme.tracker.model.Task;
+import com.acme.tracker.model.TaskStatus;
+
+import javax.servlet.http.HttpServlet;
+import javax.servlet.http.HttpServletRequest;
+import javax.servlet.http.HttpServletResponse;
+import java.io.IOException;
+
+/**
+ * Changes a task's status, and closes several tasks at once. Each action
+ * checks the task's current status is the one it expects before writing the new
+ * one.
+ */
+<span class="fc" id="L18">public class TaskActionServlet extends HttpServlet {</span>
+
+<span class="fc" id="L20">    private final TaskDao tasks = new TaskDao();</span>
+
+    @Override
+    protected void doPost(HttpServletRequest req, HttpServletResponse resp) throws IOException {
+<span class="fc" id="L24">        String action = req.getParameter(&quot;action&quot;);</span>
+
+<span class="fc bfc" id="L26" title="All 2 branches covered.">        if (&quot;bulkClose&quot;.equals(action)) {</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L27">            handleBulkClose(req, resp);</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L28">            return;</span>
+        }
+
+<span class="fc" id="L31">        long id = Long.parseLong(req.getParameter(&quot;id&quot;));</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L32">        Task t = tasks.findById(id);</span>
+<span class="fc bfc" id="L33" title="All 2 branches covered.">        if (t == null) {</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L34">            resp.sendError(HttpServletResponse.SC_NOT_FOUND, &quot;No such task&quot;);</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L35">            return;</span>
+        }
+<span class="fc" id="L37">        String current = t.getStatus();</span>
+
+<span class="pc bpc" id="L39" title="1 of 8 branches missed.">        switch (action == null ? &quot;&quot; : action) {</span>
+            case &quot;start&quot;:
+<span class="fc bfc" id="L41" title="All 2 branches covered.">                if (!guard(resp, current, TaskStatus.OPEN, action)) return;</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L42">                tasks.updateStatus(id, TaskStatus.IN_PROGRESS);</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L43">                break;</span>
+            case &quot;submit&quot;:
+<span class="fc bfc" id="L45" title="All 2 branches covered.">                if (!guard(resp, current, TaskStatus.IN_PROGRESS, action)) return;</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L46">                tasks.updateStatus(id, TaskStatus.PENDING_APPROVAL);</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L47">                break;</span>
+            case &quot;approve&quot;:
+<span class="pc bpc" id="L49" title="1 of 2 branches missed.">                if (!requireApprover(req, resp)) return;</span>
+<span class="fc bfc" id="L50" title="All 2 branches covered.">                if (!guard(resp, current, TaskStatus.PENDING_APPROVAL, action)) return;</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L51">                tasks.updateStatus(id, TaskStatus.DONE);</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L52">                break;</span>
+            case &quot;reject&quot;:
+<span class="pc bpc" id="L54" title="1 of 2 branches missed.">                if (!requireApprover(req, resp)) return;</span>
+<span class="pc bpc" id="L55" title="1 of 2 branches missed.">                if (!guard(resp, current, TaskStatus.PENDING_APPROVAL, action)) return;</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L56">                tasks.updateStatus(id, TaskStatus.IN_PROGRESS);</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L57">                break;</span>
+            case &quot;reopen&quot;:
+<span class="fc bfc" id="L59" title="All 2 branches covered.">                if (!guard(resp, current, TaskStatus.DONE, action)) return;</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L60">                tasks.updateStatus(id, TaskStatus.OPEN);</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L61">                break;</span>
+            default:
+<span class="fc" id="L63">                resp.sendError(HttpServletResponse.SC_BAD_REQUEST, &quot;Unknown action: &quot; + action);</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L64">                return;</span>
+        }
+<span class="pc bpc" id="L66" title="1 of 2 branches missed.">        if (!resp.isCommitted()) {</span>
+            // Optional: return to the focused task detail page (used by the
+            // detail view's transition buttons). Default stays the list.
+<span class="pc bpc" id="L69" title="1 of 2 branches missed.">            String target = &quot;detail&quot;.equals(req.getParameter(&quot;return&quot;))</span>
+<span class="nc" id="L70">                    ? &quot;/tasks/form?id=&quot; + id</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L71">                    : &quot;/tasks&quot;;</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L72">            resp.sendRedirect(req.getContextPath() + target);</span>
+        }
+<span class="fc" id="L74">    }</span>
+
+    private void handleBulkClose(HttpServletRequest req, HttpServletResponse resp) throws IOException {
+<span class="fc" id="L77">        String[] raw = req.getParameterValues(&quot;ids&quot;);</span>
+<span class="pc bpc" id="L78" title="1 of 4 branches missed.">        if (raw == null || raw.length == 0) {</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L79">            resp.sendRedirect(req.getContextPath() + &quot;/tasks&quot;);</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L80">            return;</span>
+        }
+<span class="fc" id="L82">        long[] ids = new long[raw.length];</span>
+<span class="fc bfc" id="L83" title="All 2 branches covered.">        for (int i = 0; i &lt; raw.length; i++) {</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L84">            ids[i] = Long.parseLong(raw[i]);</span>
+        }
+<span class="fc" id="L86">        tasks.bulkClose(ids);</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L87">        resp.sendRedirect(req.getContextPath() + &quot;/tasks&quot;);</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L88">    }</span>
+
+    /** Authorization check for the approve and reject actions. */
+    private boolean requireApprover(HttpServletRequest req, HttpServletResponse resp) throws IOException {
+<span class="fc" id="L92">        Person user = (Person) req.getSession().getAttribute(&quot;user&quot;);</span>
+<span class="pc bpc" id="L93" title="2 of 4 branches missed.">        if (user == null || !user.getRole().canApprove()) {</span>
+<span class="nc" id="L94">            resp.sendError(HttpServletResponse.SC_FORBIDDEN, &quot;Approval requires a manager or admin&quot;);</span>
+<span class="nc" id="L95">            return false;</span>
+        }
+<span class="fc" id="L97">        return true;</span>
+    }
+
+    /** Refuse the change unless the task is already in the expected status. */
+    private boolean guard(HttpServletResponse resp, String current, String expected, String action)
+            throws IOException {
+<span class="fc bfc" id="L103" title="All 2 branches covered.">        if (!expected.equals(current)) {</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L104">            resp.sendError(HttpServletResponse.SC_CONFLICT,</span>
+                    &quot;Cannot &quot; + action + &quot; a task in status &quot; + current + &quot; (expected &quot; + expected + &quot;)&quot;);
+<span class="fc" id="L106">            return false;</span>
+        }
+<span class="fc" id="L108">        return true;</span>
+    }
+}
+</pre><div class="footer"><span class="right">Created with <a href="http://www.jacoco.org/jacoco">JaCoCo</a> 0.8.12.202403310830</span></div></body></html>

+<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?><!DOCTYPE html PUBLIC "-//W3C//DTD XHTML 1.0 Strict//EN" "http://www.w3.org/TR/xhtml1/DTD/xhtml1-strict.dtd"><html xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" lang="en"><head><meta http-equiv="Content-Type" content="text/html;charset=UTF-8"/><link rel="stylesheet" href="../jacoco-resources/report.css" type="text/css"/><link rel="shortcut icon" href="../jacoco-resources/report.gif" type="image/gif"/><title>TaskFormServlet.java</title><link rel="stylesheet" href="../jacoco-resources/prettify.css" type="text/css"/><script type="text/javascript" src="../jacoco-resources/prettify.js"></script></head><body onload="window['PR_TAB_WIDTH']=4;prettyPrint()"><div class="breadcrumb" id="breadcrumb"><span class="info"><a href="../jacoco-sessions.html" class="el_session">Sessions</a></span><a href="../index.html" class="el_report">Frozen task-tracker-demo Java</a> &gt; <a href="index.source.html" class="el_package">com.acme.tracker.web</a> &gt; <span class="el_source">TaskFormServlet.java</span></div><h1>TaskFormServlet.java</h1><pre class="source lang-java linenums">package com.acme.tracker.web;
+
+import com.acme.tracker.dao.PersonDao;
+import com.acme.tracker.dao.ProjectDao;
+import com.acme.tracker.dao.TaskDao;
+import com.acme.tracker.model.Task;
+import com.acme.tracker.model.TaskStatus;
+
+import javax.servlet.ServletException;
+import javax.servlet.http.HttpServlet;
+import javax.servlet.http.HttpServletRequest;
+import javax.servlet.http.HttpServletResponse;
+import java.io.IOException;
+
+/**
+ * Creates and edits tasks. GET renders the form (blank for new, pre-filled
+ * when ?id= is present); POST validates and persists.
+ */
+<span class="fc" id="L19">public class TaskFormServlet extends HttpServlet {</span>
+
+<span class="fc" id="L21">    private final TaskDao tasks = new TaskDao();</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L22">    private final ProjectDao projects = new ProjectDao();</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L23">    private final PersonDao people = new PersonDao();</span>
+
+    @Override
+    protected void doGet(HttpServletRequest req, HttpServletResponse resp)
+            throws ServletException, IOException {
+<span class="fc" id="L28">        String idParam = req.getParameter(&quot;id&quot;);</span>
+<span class="pc bpc" id="L29" title="1 of 4 branches missed.">        if (idParam != null &amp;&amp; !idParam.isEmpty()) {</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L30">            req.setAttribute(&quot;task&quot;, tasks.findById(Long.parseLong(idParam)));</span>
+        }
+<span class="fc" id="L32">        req.setAttribute(&quot;projects&quot;, projects.findAll());</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L33">        req.setAttribute(&quot;people&quot;, people.findAll());</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L34">        req.getRequestDispatcher(&quot;/WEB-INF/jsp/task-form.jsp&quot;).forward(req, resp);</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L35">    }</span>
+
+    @Override
+    protected void doPost(HttpServletRequest req, HttpServletResponse resp)
+            throws ServletException, IOException {
+<span class="fc" id="L40">        String idParam = req.getParameter(&quot;id&quot;);</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L41">        String title = trim(req.getParameter(&quot;title&quot;));</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L42">        String projectIdParam = req.getParameter(&quot;projectId&quot;);</span>
+
+        // The title must be present (mirrors NOT NULL on task.title).
+<span class="fc bfc" id="L45" title="All 2 branches covered.">        if (title.isEmpty()) {</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L46">            fail(req, resp, &quot;Title is required&quot;);</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L47">            return;</span>
+        }
+        // The referenced project must exist; checked here as well as by the FK.
+<span class="pc bpc" id="L50" title="1 of 6 branches missed.">        if (projectIdParam == null || projectIdParam.isEmpty() || !projects.exists(Long.parseLong(projectIdParam))) {</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L51">            fail(req, resp, &quot;A task must belong to an existing project&quot;);</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L52">            return;</span>
+        }
+
+<span class="fc" id="L55">        Task t = new Task();</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L56">        t.setTitle(title);</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L57">        t.setDescription(req.getParameter(&quot;description&quot;));</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L58">        t.setPriority(orDefault(req.getParameter(&quot;priority&quot;), &quot;MEDIUM&quot;));</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L59">        t.setProjectId(Long.parseLong(projectIdParam));</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L60">        String assignee = req.getParameter(&quot;assigneeId&quot;);</span>
+<span class="fc bfc" id="L61" title="All 4 branches covered.">        t.setAssigneeId((assignee == null || assignee.isEmpty()) ? null : Long.valueOf(assignee));</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L62">        t.setDueDate(req.getParameter(&quot;dueDate&quot;));</span>
+
+        long savedId;
+<span class="pc bpc" id="L65" title="1 of 4 branches missed.">        if (idParam == null || idParam.isEmpty()) {</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L66">            t.setStatus(TaskStatus.OPEN); // new tasks always start OPEN</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L67">            savedId = tasks.create(t);</span>
+        } else {
+<span class="fc" id="L69">            savedId = Long.parseLong(idParam);</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L70">            t.setId(savedId);</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L71">            tasks.update(t);</span>
+        }
+        // Land on the just-saved task's detail page, not back on the list.
+<span class="fc" id="L74">        resp.sendRedirect(req.getContextPath() + &quot;/tasks/form?id=&quot; + savedId + &quot;&amp;saved=1&quot;);</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L75">    }</span>
+
+    private void fail(HttpServletRequest req, HttpServletResponse resp, String msg)
+            throws ServletException, IOException {
+<span class="fc" id="L79">        req.setAttribute(&quot;error&quot;, msg);</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L80">        req.setAttribute(&quot;projects&quot;, projects.findAll());</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L81">        req.setAttribute(&quot;people&quot;, people.findAll());</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L82">        req.getRequestDispatcher(&quot;/WEB-INF/jsp/task-form.jsp&quot;).forward(req, resp);</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L83">    }</span>
+
+    private static String trim(String s) {
+<span class="pc bpc" id="L86" title="1 of 2 branches missed.">        return s == null ? &quot;&quot; : s.trim();</span>
+    }
+
+    private static String orDefault(String s, String def) {
+<span class="pc bpc" id="L90" title="1 of 4 branches missed.">        return (s == null || s.isEmpty()) ? def : s;</span>
+    }
+}

+<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?><!DOCTYPE html PUBLIC "-//W3C//DTD XHTML 1.0 Strict//EN" "http://www.w3.org/TR/xhtml1/DTD/xhtml1-strict.dtd"><html xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" lang="en"><head><meta http-equiv="Content-Type" content="text/html;charset=UTF-8"/><link rel="stylesheet" href="../jacoco-resources/report.css" type="text/css"/><link rel="shortcut icon" href="../jacoco-resources/report.gif" type="image/gif"/><title>TaskListServlet.java</title><link rel="stylesheet" href="../jacoco-resources/prettify.css" type="text/css"/><script type="text/javascript" src="../jacoco-resources/prettify.js"></script></head><body onload="window['PR_TAB_WIDTH']=4;prettyPrint()"><div class="breadcrumb" id="breadcrumb"><span class="info"><a href="../jacoco-sessions.html" class="el_session">Sessions</a></span><a href="../index.html" class="el_report">Frozen task-tracker-demo Java</a> &gt; <a href="index.source.html" class="el_package">com.acme.tracker.web</a> &gt; <span class="el_source">TaskListServlet.java</span></div><h1>TaskListServlet.java</h1><pre class="source lang-java linenums">package com.acme.tracker.web;
+
+import com.acme.tracker.dao.TaskDao;
+import com.acme.tracker.model.Task;
+
+import javax.servlet.ServletException;
+import javax.servlet.http.HttpServlet;
+import javax.servlet.http.HttpServletRequest;
+import javax.servlet.http.HttpServletResponse;
+import java.io.IOException;
+import java.util.List;
+
+/**
+ * Serves the task list. With no filter params it shows every task; the
+ * status and assignee params narrow it down.
+ */
+<span class="fc" id="L17">public class TaskListServlet extends HttpServlet {</span>
+
+<span class="fc" id="L19">    private final TaskDao tasks = new TaskDao();</span>
+
+    @Override
+    protected void doGet(HttpServletRequest req, HttpServletResponse resp)
+            throws ServletException, IOException {
+<span class="fc" id="L24">        String status = req.getParameter(&quot;status&quot;);</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L25">        String assigneeParam = req.getParameter(&quot;assignee&quot;);</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L26">        String sort = req.getParameter(&quot;sort&quot;);</span>
+
+        // Route everything through search — with null filters it returns all
+        // tasks, so the chosen sort applies to the default list too. Default
+        // (no sort param) is most-recently-updated first.
+<span class="fc bfc" id="L31" title="All 4 branches covered.">        Long assigneeId = (assigneeParam == null || assigneeParam.isEmpty()) ? null : Long.valueOf(assigneeParam);</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L32">        List&lt;Task&gt; result = tasks.search(status, assigneeId, sort);</span>
+
+<span class="fc" id="L34">        req.setAttribute(&quot;tasks&quot;, result);</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L35">        req.setAttribute(&quot;statusFilter&quot;, status);</span>
+<span class="pc bpc" id="L36" title="1 of 4 branches missed.">        req.setAttribute(&quot;sortBy&quot;, (sort == null || sort.isEmpty()) ? &quot;updated&quot; : sort);</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L37">        req.getRequestDispatcher(&quot;/WEB-INF/jsp/tasks.jsp&quot;).forward(req, resp);</span>
+<span class="fc" id="L38">    }</span>
+}
+</pre><div class="footer"><span class="right">Created with <a href="http://www.jacoco.org/jacoco">JaCoCo</a> 0.8.12.202403310830</span></div></body></html>
