+"""Filter/sort family (run 02, item T-QUERY): status filter, assignee
+filter, combined filters, EVERY whitelisted sort token, default ordering,
+invalid-token fallback — asserting the ACTUAL returned row sequence parsed
+from the /tasks HTML, not just 200.
+
+Source basis (frozen ~/app @ eee783057ec28d28fb36ec05484de9b4b40ceb9c):
+- web/TaskListServlet.java:22-37 (params status/assignee/sort; empty
+  assignee -> null, i.e. NO unassigned-only filter exists)
+- dao/TaskDao.java:40-73 (dynamic WHERE) and :154-172 (orderClause
+  whitelist: status/priority/due/due_date/title/id, default updated DESC)
+Seed (seed.sql:17-21, staggered updated_at): id 1 OPEN HIGH due 06-15
+upd 09:00; id 2 IN_PROGRESS HIGH due 06-20 upd 12:00; id 3
+PENDING_APPROVAL MEDIUM due 06-10 upd 15:00; id 4 DONE LOW due NULL
+upd 18:00. Default order (updated DESC) is therefore [4,3,2,1].
+Placeholder EARS IDs (S-QRY-*/R-QRY-*) pending analyst batches.
+"""
+import re
+import subprocess
+import unittest
+import urllib.parse
+from pathlib import Path
+
+from httpcase import HttpCase
+
+OUTPUTS = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
+
+
+def reset_db():
+    subprocess.run(
+        ["python3", "harness/runtime.py", "reset"],
+        cwd=OUTPUTS, check=True, capture_output=True, text=True,
+    )
+
+
+def listed_ids(case, query=""):
+    """Actual task-id sequence rendered by GET /tasks?<query> (title links)."""
+    r = case.request("GET", "/tasks" + query)
+    case.assertEqual(200, r.status, query)
+    return [int(m) for m in re.findall(r"/tasks/form\?id=(\d+)",
+                                       r.read().decode())]
+
+
+class QueryCase(HttpCase):
+    def setUp(self):
+        super().setUp()
+        reset_db()
+        self.assertEqual(302, self.login("dev", "dev").status)
+
+
+class TestDefaultOrdering(QueryCase):
+    """S-QUERY-001 (rule R-QUERY-001): no params -> every task, most-recently-
+    updated first (TaskDao.java:171): exactly [4,3,2,1] on the seed."""
+
+    def test_s_query_001_default_order_updated_desc(self):
+        self.assertEqual([4, 3, 2, 1], listed_ids(self))
+
+
+class TestStatusFilter(QueryCase):
+    """S-QUERY-002 (rule R-QUERY-002): status=<s> narrows to exact matches.
+    CHARACTERIZATION: a non-status token (BOGUS) is not validated; the
+    query simply matches nothing -> empty list."""
+
+    def test_s_query_002_each_status_filter(self):
+        self.assertEqual([1], listed_ids(self, "?status=OPEN"))
+        self.assertEqual([2], listed_ids(self, "?status=IN_PROGRESS"))
+        self.assertEqual([3], listed_ids(self, "?status=PENDING_APPROVAL"))
+        self.assertEqual([4], listed_ids(self, "?status=DONE"))
+
+    def test_s_query_002_unknown_status_matches_nothing(self):
+        self.assertEqual([], listed_ids(self, "?status=BOGUS"))
+
+
+class TestAssigneeFilter(QueryCase):
+    """S-QUERY-003 (rule R-QUERY-003): assignee=<id> narrows to that person
+    (dev, id 3, owns tasks 1-3; admin, id 1, owns task 4).
+    CHARACTERIZATION: assignee= (empty) means NO filtering
+    (TaskListServlet.java:31) — there is no unassigned-only filter; and
+    every seeded task is assigned, so no unassigned row exists to select.
+    CHARACTERIZATION: non-numeric assignee -> OBSERVED 500 (unhandled
+    NumberFormatException at TaskListServlet.java:31; same robustness gap
+    shape as F-002 — reported as a finding candidate this run)."""
+
+    def test_s_query_003_assignee_filter(self):
+        self.assertEqual([3, 2, 1], listed_ids(self, "?assignee=3"))
+        self.assertEqual([4], listed_ids(self, "?assignee=1"))
+
+    def test_s_query_003_empty_assignee_means_no_filter(self):
+        self.assertEqual([4, 3, 2, 1], listed_ids(self, "?assignee="))
+
+    def test_s_query_003_non_numeric_assignee_observed_500(self):
+        r = self.request("GET", "/tasks?assignee=abc")
+        self.assertEqual(500, r.status)
+
+
+class TestCombinedFilter(QueryCase):
+    """S-QUERY-004 (rule R-QUERY-004): status AND assignee combine
+    (TaskDao.java:52-54 joins with AND)."""
+
+    def test_s_query_004_status_and_assignee(self):
+        self.assertEqual([1], listed_ids(self, "?status=OPEN&assignee=3"))
+        self.assertEqual([], listed_ids(self, "?status=DONE&assignee=3"))
+
+
+class TestWhitelistedSorts(QueryCase):
+    """S-QUERY-005 (rules R-QUERY-005..R-QUERY-005): every whitelisted sort token
+    yields its exact source-defined sequence on the seed
+    (TaskDao.java:154-172): priority is semantic HIGH>MEDIUM>LOW (not
+    alphabetical); due sorts ascending with NULLS LAST (PostgreSQL ASC
+    default); title breaks ties by id."""
+
+    def test_s_query_005_every_whitelisted_sort_token(self):
+        expected = {
+            "updated": [4, 3, 2, 1],   # explicit default token
+            "status": [4, 2, 1, 3],    # alphabetical + updated DESC tiebreak
+            "priority": [2, 1, 3, 4],  # semantic HIGH,MEDIUM,LOW
+            "due": [3, 1, 2, 4],       # ASC, NULLS LAST
+            "due_date": [3, 1, 2, 4],  # alias of due
+            "title": [4, 1, 3, 2],     # alphabetical, tie -> id
+            "id": [1, 2, 3, 4],
+        }
+        for token, ids in expected.items():
+            with self.subTest(sort=token):
+                self.assertEqual(ids, listed_ids(self, "?sort=" + token))
+
+
+class TestInvalidSortFallback(QueryCase):
+    """S-QUERY-006 (rule R-QUERY-006): unrecognized sort tokens — including
+    column-name/injection-shaped input — fall back to the default
+    updated-DESC clause (TaskDao.java:171); the whitelist never
+    concatenates the request value."""
+
+    def test_s_query_006_invalid_sorts_fall_back_to_default(self):
+        for bad in ("bogus", "t.title", "id; DROP TABLE task",
+                    "updated_at DESC--"):
+            with self.subTest(sort=bad):
+                q = "?" + urllib.parse.urlencode({"sort": bad})
+                self.assertEqual([4, 3, 2, 1], listed_ids(self, q))
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    unittest.main()

+"""Lifecycle + bulk family (run 02, item T-LIFE): start/submit/reopen
+transitions and their state guards, not-found/unknown/malformed requests,
+and reproductions of suspected defects F-001 (bulkClose) and F-002
+(malformed id).
+
+Source basis (frozen ~/app @ eee783057ec28d28fb36ec05484de9b4b40ceb9c):
+- web/TaskActionServlet.java:23-74 (switch, guards, redirect)
+- web/TaskActionServlet.java:76-88 + dao/TaskDao.java:128-147 (bulkClose:
+  NO canApprove check, NO starting-state guard; SQL sets every non-DONE
+  listed id to DONE) — the guard-structure difference F-001 records.
+- web/TaskActionServlet.java:31: Long.parseLong(id) before the switch ->
+  NumberFormatException -> 500 on malformed id (F-002).
+- web/AuthFilter.java:40-47: only approve/reject are role-gated, at filter
+  level, BEFORE the servlet's 404 (ordering per R-APR-002 record).
+Approve/reject success+refusal cases live in tests/test_approval_slice.py
+(inherited, non-overlapping) and are NOT repeated here.
+Seeded tasks: 1 OPEN, 2 IN_PROGRESS, 3 PENDING_APPROVAL, 4 DONE.
+Placeholder EARS IDs (S-LIF-*/R-LIF-*) pending analyst batches.
+"""
+import subprocess
+import unittest
+from pathlib import Path
+
+from httpcase import HttpCase
+
+OUTPUTS = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
+
+SEED_STATUSES = {1: "OPEN", 2: "IN_PROGRESS", 3: "PENDING_APPROVAL", 4: "DONE"}
+
+
+def reset_db():
+    subprocess.run(
+        ["python3", "harness/runtime.py", "reset"],
+        cwd=OUTPUTS, check=True, capture_output=True, text=True,
+    )
+
+
+def oracle_statuses():
+    """Read-only oracle: {id: status} from `SELECT id,status FROM task`."""
+    out = subprocess.run(
+        ["python3", "harness/runtime.py", "sql",
+         "SELECT id,status FROM task ORDER BY id"],
+        cwd=OUTPUTS, check=True, capture_output=True, text=True,
+    ).stdout
+    return {int(line.split("|")[0]): line.split("|")[1].strip()
+            for line in out.strip().splitlines()}
+
+
+class LifecycleCase(HttpCase):
+    def setUp(self):
+        super().setUp()
+        reset_db()
+
+
+class TestStartTransition(LifecycleCase):
+    """S-LIFE-001 (rule R-LIFE-001): start on OPEN task 1 (as dev — start has
+    NO role gate, TaskActionServlet.java:40-43) -> 302 /tasks, task 1
+    IN_PROGRESS. Start on task 2 (IN_PROGRESS) -> 409 'Cannot start a task
+    in status IN_PROGRESS (expected OPEN)', no mutation."""
+
+    def test_s_life_001_start_open_task(self):
+        self.assertEqual(302, self.login("dev", "dev").status)
+        r = self.task_action(1, "start")
+        self.assertEqual(302, r.status)
+        self.assertEqual("/tasks", r.headers.get("Location"))
+        self.assertEqual({**SEED_STATUSES, 1: "IN_PROGRESS"}, oracle_statuses())
+
+    def test_s_life_004_start_wrong_state_conflict(self):
+        self.assertEqual(302, self.login("dev", "dev").status)
+        r = self.task_action(2, "start")
+        self.assertEqual(409, r.status)
+        self.assertIn("Cannot start a task in status IN_PROGRESS (expected OPEN)",
+                      r.read().decode())
+        self.assertEqual(SEED_STATUSES, oracle_statuses())
+
+
+class TestSubmitTransition(LifecycleCase):
+    """S-LIFE-002 (rule R-LIFE-002): submit on IN_PROGRESS task 2 (as dev —
+    no role gate, :44-47) -> 302, task 2 PENDING_APPROVAL. Submit on OPEN
+    task 1 -> 409 '(expected IN_PROGRESS)', no mutation."""
+
+    def test_s_life_002_submit_in_progress_task(self):
+        self.assertEqual(302, self.login("dev", "dev").status)
+        r = self.task_action(2, "submit")
+        self.assertEqual(302, r.status)
+        self.assertEqual({**SEED_STATUSES, 2: "PENDING_APPROVAL"},
+                         oracle_statuses())
+
+    def test_s_life_004_submit_wrong_state_conflict(self):
+        self.assertEqual(302, self.login("dev", "dev").status)
+        r = self.task_action(1, "submit")
+        self.assertEqual(409, r.status)
+        self.assertIn("Cannot submit a task in status OPEN (expected IN_PROGRESS)",
+                      r.read().decode())
+        self.assertEqual(SEED_STATUSES, oracle_statuses())
+
+
+class TestReopenTransition(LifecycleCase):
+    """S-LIFE-003 (rule R-LIFE-003): reopen on DONE task 4 (as dev — no role
+    gate, :58-61) -> 302, task 4 OPEN. Reopen on OPEN task 1 -> 409
+    '(expected DONE)', no mutation."""
+
+    def test_s_life_003_reopen_done_task(self):
+        self.assertEqual(302, self.login("dev", "dev").status)
+        r = self.task_action(4, "reopen")
+        self.assertEqual(302, r.status)
+        self.assertEqual({**SEED_STATUSES, 4: "OPEN"}, oracle_statuses())
+
+    def test_s_life_004_reopen_wrong_state_conflict(self):
+        self.assertEqual(302, self.login("dev", "dev").status)
+        r = self.task_action(1, "reopen")
+        self.assertEqual(409, r.status)
+        self.assertIn("Cannot reopen a task in status OPEN (expected DONE)",
+                      r.read().decode())
+        self.assertEqual(SEED_STATUSES, oracle_statuses())
+
+
+class TestNotFoundAndOrdering(LifecycleCase):
+    """S-LIFE-005 (rules R-LIFE-006, R-APR-002 ordering record): approver +
+    nonexistent id -> 404 'No such task' (TaskActionServlet.java:31-36), no
+    mutation. CHARACTERIZATION (F-004 ordering cross-check, evidence/analyst/
+    fixtures.md:41-44): non-approver + nonexistent id -> 403, because the
+    filter's role check precedes the servlet's existence check."""
+
+    def test_s_life_005_approver_missing_id_404(self):
+        self.assertEqual(302, self.login("mgr", "mgr").status)
+        r = self.task_action(999, "approve")
+        self.assertEqual(404, r.status)
+        self.assertIn("No such task", r.read().decode())
+        self.assertEqual(SEED_STATUSES, oracle_statuses())
+
+    def test_s_life_005_non_approver_missing_id_403_first(self):
+        self.assertEqual(302, self.login("dev", "dev").status)
+        r = self.task_action(999, "approve")
+        self.assertEqual(403, r.status)
+        self.assertIn("Only managers or admins may approve or reject tasks",
+                      r.read().decode())
+        self.assertEqual(SEED_STATUSES, oracle_statuses())
+
+
+class TestUnknownAction(LifecycleCase):

+class TestBulkCloseMixedAndEmpty(LifecycleCase):
+    """S-BULK-002/S-BULK-001 (rule R-BULK-002; F-001-related characterization): bulkClose

+        self.assertEqual(302, r.status)

+"""Projects family (run 02, item T-PROJ): list visibility, create, validation
+refusals with no mutation, duplicate-name behavior, archive + archived
+visibility.
+
+Source basis (frozen ~/app @ eee783057ec28d28fb36ec05484de9b4b40ceb9c):
+- web/ProjectServlet.java:23-28 (GET list, all states), :31-51 (POST)
+- dao/ProjectDao.java:19-31 (findAll ORDER BY name, no state filter),
+  :57-72 (create always state ACTIVE, owner = session user),
+  :74-84 (archive: UPDATE ... WHERE state='ACTIVE' — no-op if archived)
+- db/schema.sql:18-26 (project.name NOT NULL, NO uniqueness constraint)
+Seeded projects (seed.sql:9-12): 1 Apollo ACTIVE owner 2, 2 Borealis ACTIVE
+owner 1, 3 Legacy Archive ARCHIVED owner 1.

+        cwd=OUTPUTS, check=True, capture_output=True, text=True,
+    )
+
+
+def oracle_projects():
+    """Read-only oracle: list of (id, name, state, owner_id) tuples."""
+    out = subprocess.run(
+        ["python3", "harness/runtime.py", "sql",
+         "SELECT id,name,state,owner_id FROM project ORDER BY id"],
+        cwd=OUTPUTS, check=True, capture_output=True, text=True,
+    ).stdout
+    return [tuple(line.split("|")) for line in out.strip().splitlines()]

+          ("3", "Legacy Archive", "ARCHIVED", "1")]

+class TestProjectListAfterLogin(HttpCase):

+    filter, ProjectDao.java:19-31)."""

+    def test_s_proj_001_projects_list_includes_archived(self):

+        r = self.request("GET", "/projects")
+        self.assertEqual(200, r.status)
+        body = r.read().decode()
+        for name in ("Apollo", "Borealis", "Legacy Archive"):

+class TestCreateProjectBlankNameRefused(HttpCase):

+

+        super().setUp()

+            r = self.request("POST", "/projects", {"name": bad})

+class TestDuplicateProjectName(HttpCase):
+    """S-PROJ-004 (rule R-PROJ-004) — CHARACTERIZATION (observed behavior, no

+    (schema.sql:20) and the servlet does no duplicate check, so creating a
+    second 'Apollo' succeeds and persists a duplicate row."""

+        super().setUp()
+        reset_db()
+
+    def test_s_proj_004_duplicate_name_persists_second_row(self):
+        self.assertEqual(302, self.login("mgr", "mgr").status)
+        r = self.request("POST", "/projects", {"name": "Apollo"})
+        self.assertEqual(302, r.status)
+        rows = oracle_projects()
+        self.assertEqual(SEEDED + [("4", "Apollo", "ACTIVE", "2")], rows)
+        self.assertEqual(2, [r[1] for r in rows].count("Apollo"))

+class TestArchiveProject(HttpCase):
+    """S-PROJ-005/S-PROJ-006 (rules R-PROJ-005, R-PROJ-001): POST /projects action=archive
+    id=1 -> 302; project 1 becomes ARCHIVED and REMAINS VISIBLE in the list;
+    archiving an already-archived project (id=3) is a 302 no-op
+    (ProjectDao.java:76 WHERE state='ACTIVE')."""
+
+    def setUp(self):
+        super().setUp()
+        reset_db()
+
+    def test_s_proj_005_archive_and_rearchive(self):
+        self.assertEqual(302, self.login("mgr", "mgr").status)
+        r = self.request("POST", "/projects", {"action": "archive", "id": "1"})
+        self.assertEqual(302, r.status)
+        self.assertEqual("/projects", r.headers.get("Location"))
+        self.assertEqual([("1", "Apollo", "ARCHIVED", "2")] + SEEDED[1:],
+                         oracle_projects())
+        # Archived project still listed by the UI.
+        body = self.request("GET", "/projects").read().decode()
+        self.assertIn("Apollo", body)
+        # Re-archive of an already-ARCHIVED project: 302, no state change.
+        r = self.request("POST", "/projects", {"action": "archive", "id": "3"})
+        self.assertEqual(302, r.status)
+        self.assertEqual(("3", "Legacy Archive", "ARCHIVED", "1"),
+                         oracle_projects()[2])
+

+    unittest.main()

+"""Task form family (run 02, item T-FORM): form display, create with defaults,
+edit prefill, validation refusals with no persisted row, nullable fields.
+
+Source basis (frozen ~/app @ eee783057ec28d28fb36ec05484de9b4b40ceb9c):
+- web/TaskFormServlet.java:26-35 (GET: blank form, or pre-filled via ?id=)
+- web/TaskFormServlet.java:38-75 (POST: title required; project must exist;
+  priority default MEDIUM; assignee/dueDate nullable; new tasks start OPEN;
+  redirect to /tasks/form?id=<saved>&saved=1)
+- dao/TaskDao.java:75-95 (create), :97-112 (update — does NOT touch status)
+Seeded tasks (seed.sql:17-21): ids 1..4, all assignee 3 except task 4 -> 1.
+Placeholder EARS IDs (S-FRM-*/R-FRM-*) pending analyst batches.
+"""
+import subprocess
+import unittest
+from pathlib import Path
+
+from httpcase import HttpCase
+
+OUTPUTS = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
+
+
+def reset_db():
+    subprocess.run(
+        ["python3", "harness/runtime.py", "reset"],
+        cwd=OUTPUTS, check=True, capture_output=True, text=True,
+    )
+
+
+def oracle_tasks():
+    """Read-only oracle: list of (id,title,status,priority,assignee,project,
+    due_date) tuples; NULL columns arrive as empty strings (psql -At)."""
+    out = subprocess.run(
+        ["python3", "harness/runtime.py", "sql",
+         "SELECT id,title,status,priority,assignee_id,project_id,due_date "
+         "FROM task ORDER BY id"],
+        cwd=OUTPUTS, check=True, capture_output=True, text=True,
+    ).stdout
+    return [tuple(line.split("|")) for line in out.strip().splitlines()]
+
+
+SEEDED_COUNT = 4
+
+
+class TestTaskFormDisplay(HttpCase):
+    """S-FORM-001 (rule R-FORM-001): authenticated GET /tasks/form without id
+    -> 200 'New task' form with title/project/assignee/priority fields."""
+
+    def test_s_form_001_blank_form_served(self):
+        self.assertEqual(302, self.login("dev", "dev").status)
+        r = self.request("GET", "/tasks/form")
+        self.assertEqual(200, r.status)
+        body = r.read().decode()
+        self.assertIn("New task", body)
+        for field in ('name="title"', 'name="projectId"', 'name="assigneeId"',
+                      'name="priority"', 'name="dueDate"'):
+            self.assertIn(field, body)
+
+
+class TestTaskEditPrefill(HttpCase):
+    """S-FORM-002 (rule R-FORM-002): GET /tasks/form?id=1 -> 200 pre-filled
+    with task 1's persisted values (title, project, assignee, priority,
+    due date all selected/valued per task-form.jsp EL)."""
+
+    def setUp(self):
+        super().setUp()
+        reset_db()
+
+    def test_s_form_002_edit_form_prefilled(self):
+        self.assertEqual(302, self.login("dev", "dev").status)
+        r = self.request("GET", "/tasks/form?id=1")
+        self.assertEqual(200, r.status)
+        body = r.read().decode()
+        self.assertIn('value="Design login page"', body)
+        self.assertIn('<option value="1" selected>Apollo</option>', body)
+        self.assertIn('<option value="3" selected>Devon Member</option>', body)
+        self.assertIn('<option value="HIGH" selected>HIGH</option>', body)
+        self.assertIn('value="2026-06-15"', body)
+
+
+class TestTaskCreateDefaults(HttpCase):
+    """S-FORM-003 (rules R-FORM-003, R-FORM-004): POST /tasks/form with only
+    title+projectId -> 302 to /tasks/form?id=<new>&saved=1; persisted row has
+    status OPEN (servlet-set), priority MEDIUM (default), assignee NULL,
+    due_date NULL, description NULL."""
+
+    def setUp(self):
+        super().setUp()
+        reset_db()
+
+    def test_s_form_003_create_with_defaults(self):
+        self.assertEqual(302, self.login("dev", "dev").status)
+        r = self.request("POST", "/tasks/form",
+                         {"title": "Defaults probe", "projectId": "1"})
+        self.assertEqual(302, r.status)
+        self.assertEqual("/tasks/form?id=5&saved=1", r.headers.get("Location"))
+        rows = oracle_tasks()
+        self.assertEqual(SEEDED_COUNT + 1, len(rows))
+        self.assertEqual(("5", "Defaults probe", "OPEN", "MEDIUM", "", "1", ""),
+                         rows[-1])

+class TestTaskCreateBlankTitleRefused(HttpCase):

+class TestTaskCreateInvalidProjectRefused(HttpCase):
+    """S-FORM-005 (rule R-FORM-005): missing or nonexistent projectId -> 200
+    re-render 'A task must belong to an existing project'
+    (TaskFormServlet.java:49-53); NO row persisted."""
+
+    def setUp(self):
+        super().setUp()
+        reset_db()
+
+    def test_s_form_005_missing_or_unknown_project_refused(self):
+        self.assertEqual(302, self.login("dev", "dev").status)
+        for params in ({"title": "x"}, {"title": "x", "projectId": "999"}):
+            r = self.request("POST", "/tasks/form", params)
+            self.assertEqual(200, r.status, params)
+            self.assertIn("A task must belong to an existing project",
+                          r.read().decode())
+        self.assertEqual(SEEDED_COUNT, len(oracle_tasks()))
+
+
+class TestTaskNullableRoundTrip(HttpCase):
+    """S-FORM-006 (rules R-FORM-004, R-FORM-006): nullable assignee/due_date
+    round-trip — create unassigned, edit to set assignee+due date (status
+    untouched by update, TaskDao.java:97-112), edit again clearing both back
+    to NULL (empty params -> setNull, TaskFormServlet.java:60-62).
+
+    CHARACTERIZATION note (observed, no intent claim): the edit at step 2
+    posts projectId=2, but TaskDao.update()'s SQL has no project_id column,
+    so the persisted project stays 1 — the form's project field is silently
+    dropped on edit. Reported as a finding candidate this run."""
+
+    def setUp(self):
+        super().setUp()
+        reset_db()
+
+    def test_s_form_006_nullable_fields_round_trip(self):
+        self.assertEqual(302, self.login("dev", "dev").status)
+        r = self.request("POST", "/tasks/form",
+                         {"title": "Nullables", "projectId": "1",
+                          "assigneeId": "", "dueDate": ""})
+        self.assertEqual(302, r.status)
+        row = oracle_tasks()[-1]
+        self.assertEqual(("5", "Nullables", "OPEN", "MEDIUM", "", "1", ""), row)
+        # Edit: assignee/due/priority persist; status stays OPEN; project_id
+        # OBSERVED unchanged (1) although projectId=2 was posted — update SQL
+        # simply has no project_id column (TaskDao.java:97-112).
+        r = self.request("POST", "/tasks/form",
+                         {"id": "5", "title": "Nullables", "projectId": "2",
+                          "assigneeId": "4", "dueDate": "2026-12-31",
+                          "priority": "LOW"})
+        self.assertEqual(302, r.status)
+        self.assertEqual("/tasks/form?id=5&saved=1", r.headers.get("Location"))
+        self.assertEqual(("5", "Nullables", "OPEN", "LOW", "4", "1",
+                          "2026-12-31"), oracle_tasks()[-1])
+        # Clear them again: empty strings persist SQL NULLs.
+        r = self.request("POST", "/tasks/form",
+                         {"id": "5", "title": "Nullables", "projectId": "2",
+                          "assigneeId": "", "dueDate": ""})
+        self.assertEqual(302, r.status)
+        self.assertEqual(("5", "Nullables", "OPEN", "MEDIUM", "", "1", ""),
+                         oracle_tasks()[-1])
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    unittest.main()
